   843	/**
   844	 * The keyboard pass over a page loaded fresh in the state worth checking: the first Tab is the skip link, Tab and
   845	 * Shift+Tab reach every control with a ring that contrasts, the header in drawn order, each radio group as one named
   846	 * stop that the arrow keys move within, the same in night colours, then the skip link's jump.
   847	 */
   848	async function keyboardWalk(page: Page, route: string, size: { width: number; height: number }): Promise<void> {
   849	  const skipLink = skipFor(route);
   850	  const { controls, atRest, keys, radios } = await tagControls(page);
   851	  const excused = (check: KeyCheck, desc: string) =>
   852	    KEY_EXCLUSIONS.find((e) => e.route === route && e.check === check && e.target === desc && (!e.widths || e.widths.includes(size.width)));
   853	  const used = new Set<KeyExclusion>();
   854	  const problems: string[] = [];
   855	  const note = (check: KeyCheck, desc: string, words: string) => {
   856	    const ex = excused(check, desc);
   857	    if (ex) used.add(ex);
   858	    else problems.push(`${check}: ${desc} ${words}`);
   859	  };
   860	  for (const desc of atRest) note("rest", desc, "wears a ring while not focused");
   861	
   862	  // The first Tab on a fresh page is the skip link.
   863	  await page.keyboard.press("Tab");
   864	  const first = await focusStop(page, 2000);
   865	  let start: Focused[] = [];
   866	  if (skipLink) {
   867	    const isSkip = await page.locator(skipLink.link).evaluate((el) => el === document.activeElement);
   868	    if (!isSkip) note("first", first.body ? "nothing" : first.desc, "is what the first Tab focuses, not the skip link");
   869	    // From the skip link (focused directly if the first Tab went elsewhere): forwards round the page, then back.
   870	    if (!isSkip) await page.locator(skipLink.link).focus();
   871	    const skip = await focusStop(page, 2000);
   872	    expect(skip.body, "the skip link takes focus").toBe(false);
   873	    start = [skip as Focused];
   874	    const { x, y, width, height } = start[0].box;
   875	    expect(x >= 0 && y >= 0 && x + width <= size.width && y + height <= size.height, "the skip link is wholly on screen when focused").toBe(true);
   876	  } else {
   877	    expect(first.body, "the first Tab focuses something").toBe(false);
   878	    start = [first as Focused];
   879	  }
   880	  const forward = await tabWalk(page, "Tab", controls.length + 5, start);
   881	  const backward = await tabWalk(page, "Shift+Tab", controls.length + 5);
   882	  for (const [name, stops] of [["Tab", forward], ["Shift+Tab", backward]] as const) {
   883	    const reached = new Set(stops.map((s) => s.i).filter((i) => i !== null));
   884	    controls.forEach((desc, i) => { if (!reached.has(i)) note("missed", desc, `never reached by ${name}`); });
   885	    for (const s of stops) {
   886	      if (s.i === null) note("extra", s.desc, `reached by ${name} but not counted as a control`);
   887	      if (!s.visible) note("ring", s.desc, `does not match :focus-visible going ${name}`);
   888	      for (const p of ringProblems(s.ring)) note(p.check, s.desc, `${p.words} going ${name}`);
   889	      if (s.ring.on !== "self" && !route.startsWith("/lab/")) note("ring", s.desc, `is ringed only by a wrapper going ${name}`);
   890	      if (!s.drawn) note("hidden", s.desc, `is not what is drawn at its centre going ${name}`);
   891	    }
   892	  }
   893	  for (const words of orderProblems(forward)) note("order", "header", words);
   894	  // Each radio group is one stop, entered by Tab at its checked radio (else its first), and has a name. These go
   895	  // straight to problems: no exclusion lets a radio group through.
   896	  for (const g of radios) {
   897	    const at = forward.find((s) => s.i === g.i);
   898	    if (!at) problems.push(`radio: the ${g.group} group is never reached by Tab`);
   899	    else if (at.desc !== g.desc) problems.push(`radio: Tab enters the ${g.group} group at ${at.desc}, not ${g.desc}`);
   900	    if (!backward.some((s) => s.i === g.i)) problems.push(`radio: the ${g.group} group is never reached by Shift+Tab`);
   901	    if (!g.name) problems.push(`radio: the ${g.group} group has no accessible name (a fieldset with a legend, or aria-labelledby)`);
   902	  }
   903	
   904	  // Night colours. The controls are counted again (a page with no Day/Night button is reloaded into them, which
   905	  // drops the day tags) and must be the same controls as by day, in the same order, with the same tabindex, and
   906	  // shown (not hidden, not inert). Tab and Shift+Tab each go round again and must reach every one, so a control
   907	  // that only a script can focus in night colours fails. Then each control is focused by script after a keyboard
   908	  // press (so `:focus-visible` still applies) and its ring checked for style and contrast.
   909	  await toNight(page);
   910	  const nightTags = await tagControls(page);
   911	  if (nightTags.controls.length !== controls.length) {
   912	    problems.push(`count: ${nightTags.controls.length} controls in night colours, ${controls.length} by day`);
   913	  }
   914	  for (let k = 0; k < Math.max(keys.length, nightTags.keys.length); k++) {
   915	    if (keys[k] !== nightTags.keys[k]) problems.push(`inventory: control ${k} is ${keys[k] ?? "missing"} by day, ${nightTags.keys[k] ?? "missing"} in night colours`);
   916	  }
   917	  for (const desc of nightTags.atRest) note("rest", desc, "wears a ring while not focused in night colours");
   918	  await (skipLink ? page.locator(skipLink.link) : page.locator('[data-a11y-i="0"]')).focus();
   919	  const nightFirst = await focusStop(page, 2000);
   920	  expect(nightFirst.body, "the first control takes focus in night colours").toBe(false);
   921	  const nightForward = await tabWalk(page, "Tab", nightTags.controls.length + 5, [nightFirst as Focused]);
   922	  const nightBackward = await tabWalk(page, "Shift+Tab", nightTags.controls.length + 5);
   923	  for (const [name, stops] of [["Tab", nightForward], ["Shift+Tab", nightBackward]] as const) {
   924	    const reached = new Set(stops.map((s) => s.i).filter((i) => i !== null));
   925	    nightTags.controls.forEach((desc, i) => { if (!reached.has(i)) note("missed", desc, `never reached by ${name} in night colours`); });
   926	    for (const s of stops) if (s.i === null) note("extra", s.desc, `reached by ${name} in night colours but not counted as a control`);
   927	  }
   928	  const night = await page.evaluate(() => {
   929	    const w = window as any;
   930	    let previous: HTMLElement | null = null;
    80	
    81	if (Deno.args[0] === "pond") await pondMain(Deno.args.slice(1));
    82	else await genotypeMain(Deno.args);
    83	
    84	async function genotypeMain(args: string[]) {
    85	  const a = parseArgs(args, {
    86	    string: ["dir", "subject", "rule", "step", "seed", "min-share", "twin", "out"],
    87	    boolean: ["quiet"],
    88	    default: { "min-share": "0.01" },
    89	  });
    90	  if (!a.dir) {
    91	    console.error("usage: deno run -A tools/lineage.ts --dir <bundle> [--subject HI:LO | --rule top|random|longest] [--step N] [--seed S] [--twin <bundle>] [--out file.json]");
    92	    Deno.exit(2);
    93	  }
    94	  if (a.subject && a.rule) throw new Error("--subject and --rule are exclusive");
    95	  const step = num(a.step, "step");
    96	  const ruleName = a.rule ?? "top";
    97	  let rule: SubjectRule;
    98	  if (a.subject) rule = { kind: "key", key: a.subject };
    99	  else if (ruleName === "top" || ruleName === "longest") rule = { kind: ruleName, step };
   100	  else if (ruleName === "random") {
   101	    const seed = num(a.seed, "seed");
   102	    if (seed === undefined) throw new Error("--rule random needs --seed");
   103	    rule = { kind: "random", step, seed, minShare: num(a["min-share"], "min-share")! };

codex
I found a reproducibility problem in the research instructions: they write into `/tmp/s1/` without creating it, and the tools do not create that parent directory. The soup row also records only the paused initial page and accessibility checks, which does not establish that stepping or planting works.

    return {
      toEqual(expected) {assert.deepEqual(actual, expected, message);},

224:<p><strong>What it is now.</strong> A rule-based bell that rings on a departure from neutral runs, the lab's Field notes panel with a proposer's suggestion beside each note and a "Try it here" button only a person presses, and an offline notebook. Bell v2 finished with a negative readout (<a href="https://github.com/treetopdevs/browser-life/pull/66">#66</a>); the prediction envelope for a naturalist that predicts interventions (<a href="https://github.com/treetopdevs/browser-life/pull/135">#135</a>) and its fresh-family freeze (<a href="https://github.com/treetopdevs/browser-life/pull/141">#141</a>) are merged. The walk played gradient-m3 seed 1 to step 15,000 in 13 seconds: the panel went from "Listening" to rings with one note, "Fewer living cells: 21,452 at step 10,000, against a neutral median there of 33,684", with its receipt. <code>tools/notebook.ts</code> ran on a neutral run with the full 40-run corpus present: all 40 envelope runs reproduced their record, and the neutral run rang 0 times, as it should.</p>
233:<p><strong>Not built.</strong> The proposer bench has never run (<a href="naturalist-bench-v0-2026-10-18.md">bench</a>). Notes stop at the first wound or feed, and a ring is not proof of emergence.</p>

<script>
(() => {
  const root = document.documentElement;
  const btn = document.getElementById("theme");
  const prefersDark = matchMedia("(prefers-color-scheme: dark)");
  const apply = (mode) => {
    if (mode) root.setAttribute("data-theme", mode); else root.removeAttribute("data-theme");
    const dark = mode ? mode === "dark" : prefersDark.matches;
    btn.textContent = dark ? "Day" : "Night";
  };
  let saved = null;
  try { saved = localStorage.getItem("bl-theme"); } catch {}
  apply(saved === "dark" || saved === "light" ? saved : null);
  btn.addEventListener("click", () => {
    const dark = root.getAttribute("data-theme") ? root.getAttribute("data-theme") === "dark" : prefersDark.matches;
    const next = dark ? "light" : "dark";
    apply(next);
    try { localStorage.setItem("bl-theme", next); } catch {}
  });
  prefersDark.addEventListener("change", () => { if (!root.getAttribute("data-theme")) apply(null); });
})();
</script>

   130	    // Fragment links resolve to one id each in the same file.
   224	/** Problems with walkthrough rows: unknown verdict, empty assertion, evidence that does not exist, a works row on an error page. */
   225	export function walkProblems(rows: WalkRow[], exists: (rel: string) => boolean, errorPages: Set<string>): string[] {
   226	  const out: string[] = [];
   227	  for (const r of rows) {
   228	    if (!VERDICTS.includes(r.verdict)) out.push(`${r.id}: unknown verdict "${r.verdict}"`);
   229	    if (r.assertion.length < 15) out.push(`${r.id}: no deciding assertion`);
   230	    const refs = r.evidence.match(/(?:evidence|shots)\/[\w.-]+/g) ?? [];
   231	    if (refs.length === 0) out.push(`${r.id}: no evidence reference`);
   232	    for (const ref of refs) {
   233	      if (!exists(ref)) out.push(`${r.id}: evidence ${ref} does not exist`);
   234	      if (r.verdict === "works" && errorPages.has(ref)) out.push(`${r.id}: works, but ${ref} is flagged as an error page`);
   235	    }
   236	  }
   237	  return out;
   238	}
